# Agents · R · part 20

1,026 entries, researcher to researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **researcher**: Web research agent — finds official docs, specs, ecosystem context, and primary sources to answer technical questions ([nantas/pi-config](https://github.com/nantas/pi-config/blob/HEAD/.pi/agents/researcher.md) · ★1)
- **researcher**: Documentation researcher. Given a question or topic, searches local project docs first then the web, and reports only the directly relevant findings with citations. Invoke when looking up API docs, library behavior, project decisions, ADRs, or any factual question that requires reading documentation rather than code. ([natgalla/context-historian](https://github.com/natgalla/context-historian/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude-code/agents/researcher.md) · ★1)
- **researcher**: You are the research agent. You gather, read, and synthesize evidence. ([nayutazyc-code/TJU-FYP-reviewer](https://github.com/nayutazyc-code/TJU-FYP-reviewer/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md) · ★1)
- **researcher**: You answer one research question for {{PROJECT_NAME}}. The brief gives the question, the sources to read (URLs, repositories, `REFERENCES.md` IDs), and the note path `docs/research/&lt;topic&gt;.md`. ([Nazhra05/Super-set](https://github.com/Nazhra05/Super-set/blob/HEAD/plugins/codex-content-project-workflow/templates/.codex/agents/researcher.md) · ★1)
- **researcher**: Reads external sources (repositories, web pages, documentation) for one research question and writes one cited note in docs/research/. Clones or reads the actual files instead of relying on a summary of a front page. Reports what could not be read. Returns external context — docs, gotchas, current best practices — not code. ([skadri1601/Consilium](https://github.com/skadri1601/Consilium/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md) · ★1)

Runs in isolated context so raw search results don't pollute the caller's window. Use SOMENTE quando invocado por /decide, /decide-deep ou por pedido explícito do usuário, especialmente quando a decisão depende de fatos externos. ([boot-albuquerque/epistemic-roles](https://github.com/boot-albuquerque/epistemic-roles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: **Adopt when:** evaluating libraries/services, comparing approaches, investigating unfamiliar tech, or answering "what's the best way to do X?" before a decision. ([bora168-coder/ai-engineering-os](https://github.com/bora168-coder/ai-engineering-os/blob/HEAD/.ai/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are a research agent. You can explore the codebase and search the web, but you cannot modify any files. ([bottah/agent-bobs](https://github.com/bottah/agent-bobs/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 데이터 소스·외부 API·라이브러리를 조사하고 실측 근거와 함께 보고하는 조사가(프로젝트 파일 수정 안 함 — Write는 scripts/_tmp/ 일회성 스크립트 전용). Phase 001류 작업(데이터 소스 확보, API 제약 확인, 라이선스 검토)에서 사용. 독립적인 조사 여러 건은 병렬로 여러 인스턴스를 띄운다. 메인 agent는 결론만 받고 조사 덤프를 컨텍스트에 담지 않는다. ([Brad0329/bid-collectors](https://github.com/Brad0329/bid-collectors/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Cheap web researcher — searches/scrapes current docs and returns sourced summaries ([Bram-cat/my-pi-setup](https://github.com/Bram-cat/my-pi-setup/blob/HEAD/extensions/subagents/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use before a decision that depends on material outside this conversation - product documentation, API references, competitor pages, standards, long PDFs, release notes. Returns a short summary with sources. Do NOT use for material already in the conversation, and do NOT use it to make the decision itself. ([brandonhall/claude-model-routing](https://github.com/brandonhall/claude-model-routing/blob/HEAD/plugins/model-routing/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use PROACTIVELY to research documentation, APIs, frameworks, and best practices. MUST BE USED when user mentions: "documentation for", "how does X work", API reference, research, look up, find examples, best practices. ([brewpirate/ai-barf-notes](https://github.com/brewpirate/ai-barf-notes/blob/HEAD/Claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Web researcher — searches the web and synthesizes findings into a focused, sourced brief. Dispatch it whenever you're about to teach something you're not fully certain of (a fact, name, date, formula, definition, claim), and to scope a new topic's core concepts before planning a lesson. ([briangasca/learn-claude-code](https://github.com/briangasca/learn-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researcher — 深度调研 sub-agent。多源搜索、对比分析、方案调研。报告 + sources + 搜索日志全部落盘到 ./research/ 可追溯。 ([BrikerMan/git-github.com-BrikerMan-opencrew](https://github.com/BrikerMan/git-github.com-BrikerMan-opencrew/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only investigator: reads the repository and the web, and reports what it found with sources. It cannot write files, run a shell, call MCP servers, or start other agents. Use it to look something up or map code before changing anything. ([brockshanson/agent-core](https://github.com/brockshanson/agent-core/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Badacz tylko do odczytu. Używaj, gdy trzeba zebrać fakty z kodu, dokumentacji lub sieci, zanim zapadnie decyzja. Nie modyfikuje plików. Każde twierdzenie podaje ze źródłem. ([BrokerBusiness-ai/claude-agent-kit](https://github.com/BrokerBusiness-ai/claude-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher** ([bruna-dossantos/Bruna](https://github.com/bruna-dossantos/Bruna/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 조사 전담 에이전트. 코드베이스 탐색, 버그 근본 원인 분석, 기술 선택지 비교를 수행한다. 산출물 생성이 아닌 "이해"가 목적이다. oh-my-gx:gx-tdd 구현 단계의 정체 에스컬레이션뿐 아니라 독립적으로도 호출 가능하다. &lt;example&gt; User: 로그인 실패가 간헐적으로 발생하는 원인 조사 요청 Agent: 가설 3개 수립 → git blame/log로 최근 변경 추적 → 세션 타임아웃 설정 변경이 원인으로 확인 → 조사 보고서 출력 &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; User: 결제 모듈의 구조 파악 요청 Agent: 진입점에서 시작하여 호출 체인 추적 → 레이어 구조, 핵심 클래스, 외부 연동 지점 정리 → 탐색 보고서 출력 &lt;/example&gt; ([bs-koo/oh-my-gx](https://github.com/bs-koo/oh-my-gx/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Explores a codebase to answer questions. Use when the user asks how something works, where something is defined, what calls what, or needs to understand unfamiliar code. - **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_completeness preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. - **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_depth_first preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_depth_first/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Drives /autoresearch (base skill) with the team_speed_run preset and translates findings into a trading-strategy brief. ([cajias/nautilus-competition-run](https://github.com/cajias/nautilus-competition-run/blob/HEAD/competition_3/teams/team_speed_run/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Aufklärer. Kartiert eine bestehende Codebase, findet relevante Dateien, Konventionen, Build-/Test-Kommandos und externe Doku. Read-only. - **researcher**: Web research specialist. Fans out searches, reads primary sources, cross-checks claims, and returns a tight cited summary. - **researcher**: Dış araştırma ajanı. Kütüphane/araç karşılaştırması, best practice taraması, rakip inceleme gerektiğinde kullan; bulgular .context/intel/ altına yazılır. ([Captain-jack0/QuestLog](https://github.com/Captain-jack0/QuestLog/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))

Spawned by research:deep-research, research:literature-review, research:source-comparison and research:paper-code-audit for their evidence step. Use it when a research task needs sources gathered into a file, not for a quick lookup answered in the conversation. - **researcher**: Parallel research worker for the research skill. Researches one or two commanders, strategies or themes and returns findings in the research.json contract. - **researcher**: 웹 리서치 워커 서브에이전트. 할당된 소주제에 대해 WebSearch→WebFetch로 신뢰 소스를 모아 출처 추적 가능한 구조화 findings(소주제 정리 + 주장↔소스 매핑 + 게시일·조회일 소스 목록)를 **텍스트로 반환**한다(파일을 쓰지 않음 → 메인 컨텍스트 보호). 보통 `research` 오케스트레이터 스킬이 소주제별로 병렬 디스패치한다. &lt;example&gt; Context: research 오케스트레이터가 "GraphQL vs REST 성능" 주제를 분해해 소주제를 위임 user: "소주제 'GraphQL over-fetching 실측 데이터'를 한국어로 조사해 구조화 findings로 반환해줘" assistant: "researcher 에이전트로 해당 소주제를 수집하겠습니다." &lt;commentary&gt; 오케스트레이터가 격리된 워커에 수집을 위임하는 전형적 상황 — researcher를 사용한다. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt; Context: 좁은 단일 주제라 오케스트레이터가 워커 1개만 디스패치 user: "'OpenAI structured outputs 공식 스펙'을 1차 자료 중심으로 조사해줘" assist… ([centurio1987/my-claude-plugins](https://github.com/centurio1987/my-claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-research/agents/researcher.md))

Returns facts with source URLs, never page dumps. Not for long reports or research worth keeping (use the research / deep-research skills). ([pengistudio/pengi-med-saas](https://github.com/pengistudio/pengi-med-saas/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researches one topic for pitwall's discovery or decide stage - analogues, technology options, versions, limits and prices - and returns a table where every row links a page it actually opened. Several run in parallel, each on its own slice. ([Perchusha/pitwall](https://github.com/Perchusha/pitwall/blob/HEAD/plugins/pitwall/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Local-first web researcher for routine scoping, verification, and sourced briefs ([pergioa/pi-agent-monorepo](https://github.com/pergioa/pi-agent-monorepo/blob/HEAD/config/projects/learn/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only external research role. Checks official docs and time-sensitive facts (API behavior, SDK versions, plugin policies, deprecations). Records source URLs and dates so the leader and architect can rely on the evidence. ([PeterPonyu/oh-my-grokbuild](https://github.com/PeterPonyu/oh-my-grokbuild/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Read-only researcher. Scouts the codebase (where a change belongs, which existing pattern to copy) or researches a topic on the web, and returns a short summary with file:line references and source URLs. Use from blueprint and handoff to keep code reading out of the main agent's context. ([PFCS33/my-harness](https://github.com/PFCS33/my-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use proactively whenever a task requires reading or evaluating content from external sources — web pages, search results, or fetched documents. Delegate here instead of fetching external content directly, so untrusted material is triaged before it reaches the main session. ([phamhoangquynh21-spec/ai_building](https://github.com/phamhoangquynh21-spec/ai_building/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researches one item of a separable multi-item research batch (one company, one lab) to the batch spec Main wrote. Writes only the files the spec names; never compares or ranks across items. ([PhantatP/my-ai-workflows](https://github.com/PhantatP/my-ai-workflows/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Agente de descoberta read-only. Use quando precisar mapear código existente, encontrar padrões, coletar evidências de comportamento atual ou reunir contexto antes de qualquer decisão. NÃO implementa, NÃO edita arquivos. Dispare antes do analyst quando a tarefa envolve exploração de base de código desconhecida. ([phm-aguiar/42_chat_claude](https://github.com/phm-aguiar/42_chat_claude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md) · 3 copies)
- **researcher**: Research specialist for investigating technologies, debugging issues, and gathering information. Use when evaluating tech choices, comparing libraries, or investigating complex problems. ([PhungGiaDat/Edu-platform](https://github.com/PhungGiaDat/Edu-platform/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: External researcher that searches documentation, APIs, and web sources to answer questions. Read-only — never writes or modifies files. ([pier8software/agent-blueprint](https://github.com/pier8software/agent-blueprint/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Web research specialist - returns concise summaries with sources. Use when user asks to "research", "look up", "find out about", or "investigate" a topic. Runs in background by default. ([Piotr1215/ai-coreutils](https://github.com/Piotr1215/ai-coreutils/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use when a fact must be established from docs, library source, or a URL before a decision is made. Reports verified facts with citations; anything it could not confirm is marked UNVERIFIED. ([pithpusher/claude-code-fable-orchestrator](https://github.com/pithpusher/claude-code-fable-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Investiga y documenta la evidencia externa que hace falta ANTES de que alguien decida — cómo resuelven este problema Fresha, Booksy, Square Appointments, Calendly y Cal.com; qué dicen HOY las docs de la librería, de Cloudflare o de Stripe; qué casos borde tiene el dominio de reservas; y qué señales hay de que el problema es real. Úsalo cuando falte información externa para poder planificar o diseñar, cuando alguien esté a punto de afirmar «X no se puede hacer» o «así lo hace todo el mundo», y cuando una decisión dependa de un comportamiento de plataforma que nadie ha verificado en esta sesión… ([PlezySZN/citas-saas](https://github.com/PlezySZN/citas-saas/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 코드베이스 심층 조사 및 분석 에이전트. 코드 경로 탐색, 의존성 추적, 시스템 동작 분석 전담. 코드 변경은 하지 않는다. ([POKUDING/my-harness](https://github.com/POKUDING/my-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Use before planning, to scope read-only: inventories files, locates code, summarises structure. Fast tier, cannot write anything, answers with paths and line numbers. ([policyon/keel](https://github.com/policyon/keel/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md) · 2 copies)
- **researcher**: You sit between the cheap-tier `research_assistant` (archive-only) and the expensive escalation tier `deep_researcher` (live external sources). Your single job is to read the cheap-tier output and decide whether it answers the architect's research questions well enough. ([PolyphonyRequiem/polyphony](https://github.com/PolyphonyRequiem/polyphony/blob/HEAD/.polyphony-config/agent-guidance/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Gathers and analyses the material behind a requirement — Confluence pages, Jira history, filed Microsoft intake, and outside research via Gemini — and returns a findings brief with the gaps named. Use as the first stage of solutioning, or whenever a question needs evidence gathered before an opinion is worth having. ([poornimaka99x/fde-local-workspace](https://github.com/poornimaka99x/fde-local-workspace/blob/HEAD/fde-setup/fde-toolkit/plugins/fde-core/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are Glorp's research subagent. ([porkytheblack/glorp](https://github.com/porkytheblack/glorp/blob/HEAD/src/agent/prompts/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are the **researcher** subagent. Your job is to map every entry in `port-plan.md`'s `findings` section to a target-harness equivalent. You write your output back into the same `port-plan.md` — into the `mappings`, `silent_assumptions`, and `open_questions` sections. ([pottedmeat/skills](https://github.com/pottedmeat/skills/blob/HEAD/plugin-porter/references/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: 技术研究执行 Agent。负责执行具体检索和研究任务，根据 research-ask 的追问方向补充研究。 专注于从 DeepWiki 和网络资源中获取技术文档和代码仓库信息。 【不由用户直接调用，由 research-ask 调度】 ([PPsteven/deepresearch](https://github.com/PPsteven/deepresearch/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researches ONE chapter topic into a verified dossier. Use at PIPELINE step 1 (driven by /research). Takes a CANDIDATE_POOL key, surveys the pinned the pinned authority set (00-strategy/SOURCE-PIN.md) (the pins in SOURCE-PIN.md) + reputable sources, and banks a dossier filled against RESEARCH-TEMPLATE. Parallel-capable across topics; never bulk — one topic per run. ([Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality](https://github.com/Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Researches ONE chapter topic into a verified dossier. Use at PIPELINE step 1 (driven by /research). Takes a CANDIDATE_POOL key, surveys the pinned {{AUTHORITY_SOURCE}} ({{AUTHORITY_PIN}}) + reputable sources, and banks a dossier filled against RESEARCH-TEMPLATE. Parallel-capable across topics; never bulk — one topic per run. ([Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality](https://github.com/Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality/blob/HEAD/.foundation/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: You are a methodologist with fifteen-plus years in applied statistics — clinical trials, A/B testing, time series forecasting, the whole span of "does this effect actually exist and can we trust the number we're putting on it." You are, first and foremost, a reviewer. Other people build the pipeline; you make sure the pipeline's statistics hold up before anyone bets a decision on them. ([proflandrigan/emergent-flow](https://github.com/proflandrigan/emergent-flow/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Web researcher — verifies facts, formulas and conventions (course slides first), synthesizes a sourced brief. Runs in Claude Code. ([promaaa/learn](https://github.com/promaaa/learn/blob/HEAD/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Verifies a fact, formula, sign, definition or convention before the tutor teaches it, or scopes a topic (first principles, standard framings, common gotchas) before a lesson is planned. Checks the course slides and the web, and returns a short sourced brief. ([promaaa/learn](https://github.com/promaaa/learn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
- **researcher**: Haiku swarm for external documentation lookup (Rust crate docs and source, GitHub Actions and REST API, Hugging Face Hub API and model cards, the MCP spec, Helm, SQLite). ([prosperity-solutions/firelight](https://github.com/prosperity-solutions/firelight/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/researcher.md))
